## A2a

### contactRules.create

`a2a.contactRules.create`

Creates an allow or block rule (`action`) controlling which agent (`match_target`) can exchange A2A tasks with this identity, optionally per `direction`. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contactRules.create({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', action: 'allow', match_target: 'example.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block` | Yes | — |
| `match_type` | `handle` | No | — |
| `match_target` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_target` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### contactRules.delete

`a2a.contactRules.delete`

Deletes one A2A contact rule (`rule_id`) from an identity. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contactRules.delete({ rule_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rule_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### contactRules.list

`a2a.contactRules.list`

Lists the A2A allow and block rules for one identity (`agent_handle`).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contactRules.list({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  action?: string | null,
  match_type?: string | null,
  match_target?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### contactRules.listForOrg

`a2a.contactRules.listForOrg`

Lists active A2A contact rules across every identity in the organization, filterable by `agent_identity_id`, `action` and `direction`. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contactRules.listForOrg({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both \| null` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `offset` | `number` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  action?: string | null,
  match_type?: string | null,
  match_target?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null,
  agent_identity_id?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### contactRules.update

`a2a.contactRules.update`

Changes an A2A contact rule's `action` and/or `direction`; to change the match target, delete and recreate the rule. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contactRules.update({ rule_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rule_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_target` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### contexts.get

`a2a.contexts.get`

Returns one A2A context (a conversation session) that another agent opened toward this identity, with its tasks in both directions.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contexts.get({ context_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `context_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `caller` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `target` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `tasks` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `tasks_truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_activity_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="caller full type">

```ts
{
  identity_id?: string | null,
  organization_id?: string | null,
  handle?: string | null | null,
  trust_tier?: string | null,
  affiliation?: {
    domain?: string | null,
    verifier?: string | null,
    last_success_at?: string | null,
    valid_until?: string | null
  } | null | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

### contexts.update

`a2a.contexts.update`

Renames an A2A context (`name`). Either participant can rename it, whoever opened it.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.contexts.update({ context_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent', name: 'Support inbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `context_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `caller` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `target` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `tasks` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `tasks_truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_activity_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### invitations.accept

`a2a.invitations.accept`

Accepts an A2A invitation (`invitation_token`) as the agent that owns the calling agent-scoped API key, connecting it to the invitation's peer agents.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.invitations.accept({ invitation_token: 'inv_tok_example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_token` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `invitee_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `invitee_agent_handle` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `peer_agent_handles` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `accepted_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### invitations.create

`a2a.invitations.create`

Creates an A2A invitation that lets an outside agent connect to `peer_agent_handles`; Inkbox emails `recipient_email` if set, otherwise returns a link to hand off. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.invitations.create({
	peer_agent_handles: ['example'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `peer_agent_handles` | `string[]` | Yes | — |
| `recipient_email` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `expires_in_seconds` | `number` | No | — |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  issuer_organization_id?: string | null,
  inviter_email?: string | null | null,
  peer_agent_handles?: string[] | null,
  recipient_email?: string | null | null,
  status?: string | string | null,
  invitee_identity_id?: string | null | null,
  invitee_agent_handle?: string | null | null,
  invitee_organization_id?: string | null | null,
  email_status?: string | null,
  email_sent_at?: string | null | null,
  expires_at?: string | null,
  accepted_at?: string | null | null,
  declined_at?: string | null | null,
  revoked_at?: string | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null,
  invitation_token?: string | null,
  invitation_url?: string | null,
  agent_handoff_prompt?: string | null
} | {
  id?: string | null,
  issuer_organization_id?: string | null,
  inviter_email?: string | null | null,
  peer_agent_handles?: string[] | null,
  recipient_email?: string | null | null,
  status?: string | string | null,
  invitee_identity_id?: string | null | null,
  invitee_agent_handle?: string | null | null,
  invitee_organization_id?: string | null | null,
  email_status?: string | null,
  email_sent_at?: string | null | null,
  expires_at?: string | null,
  accepted_at?: string | null | null,
  declined_at?: string | null | null,
  revoked_at?: string | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### invitations.decline

`a2a.invitations.decline`

Declines an A2A invitation by its `invitation_token`. Connections already accepted are not affected. Inkbox needs no API key for this route; the plugin still sends the connected one.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.invitations.decline({ invitation_token: 'inv_tok_example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_token` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

`a2a.invitations.get`

Returns one A2A invitation issued by your organization, with its status, peer agents and email delivery state.

**Risk:** `read`


|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| `issuer_organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inviter_email` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `peer_agent_handles` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `recipient_email` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| string \| null` | No | — |
| `invitee_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `invitee_agent_handle` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `invitee_organization_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `email_status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `email_sent_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `expires_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `accepted_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `declined_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `revoked_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### invitations.preview

`a2a.invitations.preview`

Shows an A2A invitation's inviter, peer agents and expiry from the raw `invitation_token` without accepting it. Inkbox needs no API key for this route; the plugin still sends the connected one.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.invitations.preview({ invitation_token: 'inv_tok_example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_token` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `inviter_email` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `peer_agent_handles` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `expires_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_handoff_prompt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### invitations.revoke

`a2a.invitations.revoke`

Revokes a pending or awaiting-verification A2A invitation so it can no longer be accepted. Accepted, declined or expired invitations cannot be revoked.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.a2a.invitations.revoke({ invitation_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `invitation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `issuer_organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inviter_email` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `peer_agent_handles` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `recipient_email` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| string \| null` | No | — |

## Api Keys

### create

`apiKeys.create`

Creates an API key (`label`): set `scoped_identity_id` for an agent-scoped key, or omit it for an admin key with full organization access. The key value is returned only once.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.apiKeys.create({ label: 'support-agent key', scoped_identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `label` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `scoped_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | Scope this key to a specific agent identity. Omit or null for an admin (unscoped) key with full org-wide authority. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `api_key` | `string \| null` | No | Full API key string. Shown once, cannot be retrieved later. |
| `record` | `object \| null` | No | — |

```ts
} | null
```

### changePlan

`billing.changePlan`

Changes the organization's subscription `plan` (`free` cancels) and optionally its billing `interval`; requires an admin key. Upgrades and moves to yearly are charged and prorated immediately.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.changePlan({ plan: 'free' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `plan` | `free \| hobbyist \| developer \| startup \| enterprise` | Yes | — |
| `startup_variant` | `outbound \| null` | No | Startup only: pass the fixed value 'outbound' when selecting the supported $200 plan. Omit or pass null while keeping the current Startup subscription to clear a pending change without changing its legacy price. |
| `interval` | `month \| year \| null` | No | Plan fee renewal cadence to switch to: 'month' or 'year'. Omit to keep the current cadence. Moving to yearly takes effect immediately (prorated); moving from yearly to monthly takes effect at the yearly renewal. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `billing_account_available` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether payment-method and invoice management is available. |
| `plan` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Stripe subscription status (active, past_due, ...) or None on Free. |
| `cancel_at_period_end` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the subscription is set to cancel at the period boundary. |
| `current_period_end` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the current monthly usage cycle ends, if subscribed. Allowances reset and usage is billed at this boundary. |
| `billing_interval` | `string \| null \| null` | No | How often the plan fee renews ('month' or 'year'); None on Free. |
| `plan_period_end` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the plan fee next renews. Equals current_period_end on monthly plans; up to a year out on yearly plans. |
| `pending_plan` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Plan the subscription will switch to at period end (FREE = scheduled cancel), if any. |
| `pending_change_date` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the pending plan change / cancellation takes effect. |
| `pending_startup_variant` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the pending plan is Startup: which flat-price variant the scheduled switch lands on. None otherwise. |
| `pending_billing_interval` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Renewal cadence of the pending paid plan, if a switch is scheduled. |
| `legacy_developer_price` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when the subscription's flat price is the retired $25 Developer price (sunset migration pending); the console surfaces the phase-out banner from this. |
| `stripe_configured` | `boolean \| null` | No | False when Stripe isn't configured server-side; the UI hides upgrade actions. |
| `annual_billing_available` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether yearly plan billing can be selected. |
| `balance` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Stripe customer account balance in cents (negative = credit available, positive = amount owed). None on Free with no customer yet, or if the read failed. |
| `estimated_next_bill` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Amount due in cents on the upcoming invoice (plan fee + metered overage accrued so far this cycle), net of any account credit. None when there's no subscription (Free) or the preview read failed. |
| `mail_custom_signatures_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether custom email signatures are available to this organization, including account-specific overrides, regardless of subscription status. |
| `entitlements` | `object \| null` | No | Resolved organization entitlements, including additional grants on Free. May be absent from plan-change responses. |
| `lapsed_subscription` | `object \| null` | No | On Free only: a plan subscription that is unpaid, incomplete or paused and still needs the customer to act. Informational; it grants no plan, and `plan` and `status` keep describing the organization's effective billing state. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="entitlements full type">

```ts
{
  is_custom?: boolean | null,
  display_name?: string | null,
  plan_amount_cents?: number | null | null,
  plan_amount_interval?: string | null | null,
  phone_numbers?: number | null,
  sending_domains?: number | null,
  identities?: number | null | null,
  mail_custom_signatures_enabled?: boolean | null,
  mail_storage_bytes_per_identity?: number | null,
```

### createCheckoutSession

`billing.createCheckoutSession`

Creates a Stripe Checkout URL for subscribing to a `plan`, returning to `success_url` or `cancel_url`. The user pays in the browser.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.createCheckoutSession({ plan: 'free', success_url: 'https://example.com/inkbox', cancel_url: 'https://example.com/inkbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `plan` | `free \| hobbyist \| developer \| startup \| enterprise` | Yes | — |
| `startup_variant` | `outbound` | No | Startup only: the supported $200 dedicated-line option. The fixed value is 'outbound'; ignored for other plans. |
| `interval` | `month \| year` | No | — |
| `success_url` | `string` | Yes | Absolute URL Stripe returns to after a completed checkout. Must be https on an inkbox.ai host (localhost allowed in dev). |
| `cancel_url` | `string` | Yes | Absolute URL Stripe returns to if the user cancels checkout. Same host restrictions as success_url. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | Stripe Checkout session URL. |



---

### createPortalSession

`billing.createPortalSession`

Creates a Stripe billing portal URL where a person manages the organization's billing, returning to `return_url` afterwards.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.createPortalSession({ return_url: 'https://example.com/inkbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `return_url` | `string` | Yes | Absolute URL Stripe returns to when the user leaves the billing portal. Must be https on an inkbox.ai host (localhost allowed in dev). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | Stripe billing portal session URL. |

### getSubscription

`billing.getSubscription`

Returns the organization's plan, status, billing interval, pending changes and entitlements, including whether custom email signatures are available.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.getSubscription({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `billing_account_available` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether payment-method and invoice management is available. |
| `plan` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Stripe subscription status (active, past_due, ...) or None on Free. |
| `cancel_at_period_end` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the subscription is set to cancel at the period boundary. |
| `current_period_end` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the current monthly usage cycle ends, if subscribed. Allowances reset and usage is billed at this boundary. |
| `billing_interval` | `string \| null \| null` | No | How often the plan fee renews ('month' or 'year'); None on Free. |
| `plan_period_end` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the plan fee next renews. Equals current_period_end on monthly plans; up to a year out on yearly plans. |
| `pending_plan` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Plan the subscription will switch to at period end (FREE = scheduled cancel), if any. |
| `pending_change_date` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds when the pending plan change / cancellation takes effect. |
| `pending_startup_variant` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the pending plan is Startup: which flat-price variant the scheduled switch lands on. None otherwise. |
| `pending_billing_interval` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Renewal cadence of the pending paid plan, if a switch is scheduled. |
| `legacy_developer_price` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when the subscription's flat price is the retired $25 Developer price (sunset migration pending); the console surfaces the phase-out banner from this. |
| `stripe_configured` | `boolean \| null` | No | False when Stripe isn't configured server-side; the UI hides upgrade actions. |
| `annual_billing_available` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether yearly plan billing can be selected. |
| `balance` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Stripe customer account balance in cents (negative = credit available, positive = amount owed). None on Free with no customer yet, or if the read failed. |
| `estimated_next_bill` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Amount due in cents on the upcoming invoice (plan fee + metered overage accrued so far this cycle), net of any account credit. None when there's no subscription (Free) or the preview read failed. |
| `mail_custom_signatures_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether custom email signatures are available to this organization, including account-specific overrides, regardless of subscription status. |
| `entitlements` | `object \| null` | No | Resolved organization entitlements, including additional grants on Free. May be absent from plan-change responses. |
| `lapsed_subscription` | `object \| null` | No | On Free only: a plan subscription that is unpaid, incomplete or paused and still needs the customer to act. Informational; it grants no plan, and `plan` and `status` keep describing the organization's effective billing state. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="entitlements full type">

```ts
{
  is_custom?: boolean | null,
  display_name?: string | null,
  plan_amount_cents?: number | null | null,

  phone_numbers?: number | null,
  sending_domains?: number | null,
  identities?: number | null | null,
```

### getUsage

`billing.getUsage`

Returns organization usage for the current billing cycle (SMS/MMS, call seconds, iMessage, storage, identities) by channel and identity, with remaining allowances and overage.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.getUsage({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `storage` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `cycle_start` | `number \| null` | No | Unix seconds for the start of the current usage cycle. |
| `cycle_end` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Unix seconds for the end of the current usage cycle (Stripe period end, or the calendar-month boundary for orgs with no subscription). |
| `plan` | `string \| null` | No | Org's current plan (free/hobbyist/developer/startup/enterprise). |
| `channels` | `object[] \| null` | No | Org-wide totals, one per channel with usage this cycle. |
| `identities` | `object[] \| null` | No | Per-identity usage breakdown. |
| `sms_allowance_per_identity` | `number \| null` | No | Deprecated compatibility field; zero because allowances are shared by the organization. |
| `voice_sec_allowance_per_identity` | `number \| null` | No | Deprecated compatibility field; zero because allowances are shared by the organization. |
| `allowance_scope` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_allowance_per_org_per_cycle` | `number \| null` | No | SMS/MMS included across the organization per billing cycle. |
| `voice_sec_allowance_per_org_per_cycle` | `number \| null` | No | Voice seconds included across the organization per billing cycle. |
| `sms_allowance_used` | `number \| null` | No | Debits against the shared SMS/MMS allowance. |
| `voice_sec_allowance_used` | `number \| null` | No | Debits against the shared voice allowance, in seconds. |
| `sms_allowance_remaining` | `number \| null` | No | Shared included SMS/MMS remaining. |
| `voice_sec_allowance_remaining` | `number \| null` | No | Shared included voice seconds remaining, before minute packs. |
| `sms_allowance_credit` | `number \| null` | No | Additional included SMS/MMS preserved for this cycle. |
| `voice_sec_allowance_credit` | `number \| null` | No | Additional included voice seconds preserved for this cycle. |
| `overage_cap_cents` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Org's monthly SMS+voice overage spend limit in cents; None when unset. |
| `overage_spend_cents` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Contract-priced SMS and voice overage in cents; null when historical pricing is unavailable. |
| `imessage_shared_messages` | `number \| null` | No | Shared-pool iMessage messages this cycle (both directions) — the count the plan's shared iMessage cap is enforced against. |
| `imessage_dedicated_messages` | `number \| null` | No | Dedicated-line iMessage messages this cycle (both directions) — the count the dedicated-line cap is enforced against. |
| `imessage_messages_per_org_per_cycle` | `number \| null` | No | Resolved shared-service iMessage message limit per billing cycle; zero means no allowance. |
| `imessage_dedicated_messages_per_org_per_cycle` | `number \| null` | No | Resolved dedicated-line iMessage message limit per billing cycle; zero means no allowance. |
| `imessage_unique_recipients` | `number \| null` | No | Unique shared-pool iMessage recipients this cycle. Reported only; recipients are capped per agent when they connect. |
| `identities_used` | `number \| null` | No | Non-deleted agent identities in the org — the count the plan's identity cap is enforced against on create. |
| `identities_included` | `number \| null` | No | Effective identity capacity, including grants and covered identity packs. |
| `identities_baseline` | `number \| null` | No | Resolved identity capacity before identity packs. |
| `identity_pack_slots` | `number \| null` | No | Additional identity slots with paid or renewal-processing coverage. |
| `voice_pack_sec_granted` | `number \| null` | No | Voice seconds available this cycle from minute packs, shared across the org. Drawn after each identity's included allowance and before overage. |
| `voice_pack_sec_used` | `number \| null` | No | Minute-pack voice seconds used so far this cycle. |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
  items?: {
    id?: string | null,

  }[] | null,
  next_cursor?: string | null | null,
  used_bytes?: number | null,

} | null | null
```

### lineBundles.quote

`billing.lineBundles.quote`

Quotes the prorated price and credits of a line bundle (`code`) and returns a `quote` token for buying it. Does not charge.

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.lineBundles.quote({ code: 'voip_line_bundle' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `expires_at` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `code` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `monthly_amount` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `bundle_amount_cents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `amount_due_cents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `invoice_total_cents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `currency` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `voice_seconds` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `messages` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `period_end` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### settings.get

`billing.settings.get`

Returns the organization's monthly SMS and voice overage spending cap and whether the 'Sent via Inkbox' email footer is removed.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.settings.get({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `overage_cap_cents` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Monthly SMS+voice overage spend limit in cents; None = no limit. Once cycle-to-date overage spend reaches it, overage-incurring sends and calls are refused (402) until the cycle resets or the limit is raised. Usage within included allowances is never blocked; inbound and in-flight usage can exceed the limit slightly. |
| `remove_branding_footer` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true and the organization is on a paid plan, outbound emails omit the 'Sent via Inkbox' footer. Automatically set to true when upgrading from Free to any paid plan. On the Free plan the footer always applies. |



---

### settings.update

`billing.settings.update`

Sets the monthly SMS/voice overage cap (`overage_cap_cents`; null clears it, 0 blocks overage) and/or `remove_branding_footer` (paid plans only).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.settings.update({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `overage_cap_cents` | `number \| null` | No | Monthly SMS+voice overage spend limit in cents. Omit to leave unchanged; null clears the limit; 0 blocks all overage-incurring sends and calls. |
| `remove_branding_footer` | `boolean \| null` | No | Set true to remove the 'Sent via Inkbox' footer from outbound emails (paid plans only; 403 on Free). Omit or null to leave unchanged. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `overage_cap_cents` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Monthly SMS+voice overage spend limit in cents; None = no limit. Once cycle-to-date overage spend reaches it, overage-incurring sends and calls are refused (402) until the cycle resets or the limit is raised. Usage within included allowances is never blocked; inbound and in-flight usage can exceed the limit slightly. |
| `remove_branding_footer` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true and the organization is on a paid plan, outbound emails omit the 'Sent via Inkbox' footer. Automatically set to true when upgrading from Free to any paid plan. On the Free plan the footer always applies. |

### setVoicePacks

`billing.setVoicePacks`

Sets how many call-minute packs of one size the organization holds (`pack`, `quantity`; 0 removes them); increases are invoiced immediately. Paid plans and admin key only.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.setVoicePacks({ pack: 'minutes_250', quantity: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `pack` | `minutes_250 \| minutes_2000` | Yes | — |
| `quantity` | `number` | Yes | Desired number of this pack; 0 removes it. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `packs` | `object[] \| null` | No | Every pack size with the org's quantity. |
| `voice_pack_sec_granted` | `number \| null` | No | Voice seconds available this cycle from minute packs. |
| `voice_pack_sec_used` | `number \| null` | No | Minute-pack voice seconds used so far this cycle. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="packs full type">

```ts
{
  pack?: string | null,
  minutes?: number | null,
  price_cents?: number | null,
  quantity?: number | null,
  ending_quantity?: number | null | null,
  ending_at?: number | null | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### usageAlerts.save

`billing.usageAlerts.save`

Creates or updates a usage email alert (`rule_id`; a new UUID adds one) for a `metric` at a percentage `threshold`, optionally for one `mailbox_id` (storage).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.billing.usageAlerts.save({ rule_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', metric: 'identities', threshold: 100 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rule_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `metric` | `identities \| storage \| sms \| voice \| overage` | Yes | — |
| `threshold` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `mailbox_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `metric` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `threshold` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `mailbox_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `is_default` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### brand.register

`compliance.brand.register`

Registers the organization's 10DLC brand (business details for US SMS) for verification. One brand per organization; asynchronous, so check its status afterwards.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.compliance.brand.register({
	entity_type: 'PRIVATE_PROFIT',
	display_name: 'example',
	email: 'ada@example.com',
	vertical: 'PROFESSIONAL',
	country: 'US',
	phone: '+14155550123',
	website: 'example',
	street: 'example',
	city: 'example',
	state: 'example',
	postal_code: 'example',
});
```

### brand.sendSmsOtp

`compliance.brand.sendSmsOtp`

Texts a one-time PIN to the brand's phone to verify a sole-proprietor 10DLC brand; confirm it with `compliance.brand.verifySmsOtp`.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.compliance.brand.sendSmsOtp({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `brand_id` | `string \| null` | No | The registry-side brand UUID the OTP was triggered for. |
| `reference_id` | `string \| null` | No | Carrier-registry reference id for this OTP attempt. Useful for debugging if a customer reports they never received the SMS. |

### campaign.register

`compliance.campaign.register`

Submits the organization's 10DLC campaign (use case, sample messages, opt-in/opt-out keywords, policy links) for carrier review. Costs $20/month once approved.

### uploadOptInEvidence

`compliance.uploadOptInEvidence`

Uploads an image (`file`, max 5 MiB) of your SMS opt-in flow and returns a public URL to cite in the campaign's `message_flow`.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.compliance.uploadOptInEvidence({
	file: { base64: 'iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==', filename: 'avatar.png', contentType: 'image/png' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `file` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | A file: a public URL to it, or &#123;url, filename?, contentType?&#125;, or &#123;base64, filename?, contentType?&#125; for a small one. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="file full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Contacts

### bulkDelete

`contacts.bulkDelete`

Deletes 1 to 1,000 contacts by `contact_ids` and returns a result for each.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.bulkDelete({
	contact_ids: ['7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7'],
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_ids` | `string[]` | Yes | — |

| `error_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `results` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

### communicationPolicy.preview

`contacts.communicationPolicy.preview`

Previews what one agent identity (`identity_id`) can see of a contact and whether it may email or phone it in each direction. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.communicationPolicy.preview({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

### communicationPolicy.set

`contacts.communicationPolicy.set`

Sets which of a contact's `addresses` an agent identity (`identity_id`) may use inbound and outbound, and its `visibility`, given the current `expected_revision`. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.communicationPolicy.set({
	contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	expected_revision: 1,
	identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	addresses: [{ kind: 'email', value: 'example', action: 'inherit' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `addresses` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `visibility` | `object \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  kind: email | phone,
  value: string,
  action: inherit | allow | block,
  direction?: inbound | outbound | both,
  expected_action?: inherit | allow | block | null,
  expected_inbound_action?: inherit | allow | block | null,
  expected_outbound_action?: inherit | allow | block | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="visibility full type">

```ts
    profile: inherit | allow | block,

    identity_id: string
  }[]
} | null
```

</AccordionGroup>

| `visibility` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### create

`contacts.create`

Creates a contact with names, company, emails, phones, addresses and custom fields. At least one name field or `company_name` is required.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.create({
	given_name: 'Ada',
	family_name: 'Lovelace',
	emails: [{ value: 'ada@example.com', is_primary: true }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `preferred_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name_prefix` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `given_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `middle_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `family_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name_suffix` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `company_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `job_title` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `birthday` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `notes` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `emails` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `phones` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `websites` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `dates` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `addresses` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `custom_fields` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `access_identity_ids` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated. Non-null values are rejected; use contact communication policies instead. |

### createWithPermissions

`contacts.createWithPermissions`

Creates a contact and sets one agent's access `permissions` in a single transaction. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.createWithPermissions({
	permissions: { identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' },
});
```

### delete

`contacts.delete`

Deletes one contact by `contact_id`. Use `contacts.bulkDelete` to delete up to 1,000 at once.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.delete({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### exportVcard

`contacts.exportVcard`

Downloads one contact (`contact_id`) as a vCard (.vcf) file.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.exportVcard({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### exportVcards

`contacts.exportVcards`

Exports 1 to 25 contacts (`contact_ids`) as a single vCard document.

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.exportVcards({
	contact_ids: ['7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7'],
});
```

| `contact_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `vcard` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### facts.create

`contacts.facts.create`

Adds a memory (`content`, `kind`) to a contact. Requires an admin API key; agent-scoped keys cannot write memories.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.facts.create({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', content: 'Prefers email over phone calls.', kind: 'profile' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `content` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `kind` | `profile \| preference \| context` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `contact_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `content` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `confidence` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `origin` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `kind` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `expires_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `locked_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `citations` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

### facts.delete

### getCorrespondence

`contacts.getCorrespondence`

Returns email, SMS, iMessage, WhatsApp and call history between one contact and an agent identity, filterable by `channels`, `after`/`before` and per-channel limits.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.getCorrespondence({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `channels` | `string[]` | No | Channels to include. Repeat the parameter or use comma-separated values. |
| `after` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `before` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `limit_per_channel` | `number` | No | — |
| `email_limit` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_limit` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `imessage_limit` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `calls_limit` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `whatsapp_limit` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `content` | `metadata \| preview \| full` | No | — |
| `transcripts` | `none \| abridged \| full` | No | — |
| `include_failed` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `channels` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### import

`contacts.import`

Imports up to 2,500 contacts from a vCard body (max 10 MiB). Pass `X-VCard-Preview-Hash` from a preview to fail if the data or conflicts changed.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.import({ vcard: 'BEGIN:VCARD
VERSION:3.0
FN:Ada Lovelace
EMAIL:ada@example.com
END:VCARD
' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `vcardPreviewHash` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `vcard` | `string` | Yes | The vCard text to import: one or more cards, UTF-8, at most 10 MiB and 2,500 cards. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `error_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `results` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  } | null | null,

}[] | null
```

### lookup

`contacts.lookup`

Finds contacts by one field: exact `email` or `phone`, or partial match with `email_contains`, `email_domain` or `phone_contains`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.lookup({ email: 'ada@example.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `email` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `email_contains` | `string \| null` | No | Substring match on stored email value (case-insensitive). |
| `email_domain` | `string \| null` | No | Suffix match on '@&lt;domain&gt;'. Leading '@' is tolerated. |
| `phone` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phone_contains` | `string \| null` | No | Substring match on stored E.164 phone value. |

### merge

`contacts.merge`

Merges duplicate contacts (`losing_contact_ids`) into this contact, combining identifiers, correspondence and memories. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.merge({
	contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	losing_contact_ids: ['7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `losing_contact_ids` | `string[]` | Yes | — |
| `field_sources` | `Record<string, string>` | No | — |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `preferred_name` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `name_prefix` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `given_name` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `middle_name` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### previewImport

`contacts.previewImport`

Validates a vCard batch (`vcard`) and reports conflicts with existing contacts, without creating any. Returns a `preview_hash` for the import.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.contacts.previewImport({ vcard: 'BEGIN:VCARD
VERSION:3.0
FN:Ada Lovelace
EMAIL:ada@example.com
END:VCARD
' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `vcard` | `string` | Yes | — |

| `conflicts` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `payload_hash` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `preview_hash` | `string \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  contact_ids?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```

### update

`contacts.update`

Updates a contact with JSON merge-patch: sent fields replace, null clears, omitted fields are kept.

**Risk:** `write`

## Domains

### create

`domains.create`

Registers a custom email sending domain (`domain`) and returns the DNS records to publish. An apex with existing MX records needs `apex_mx_acknowledged` to displace that mail provider.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.domains.create({ domain: 'mail.example.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `domain` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `apex_mx_acknowledged` | `boolean` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `domain` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `dns_records` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `apex_mx_warning` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `spf_lookup_report` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="domain full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  organization_id?: string | null,
  domain?: string | null,
  mail_from_subdomain?: string | null,
  is_default?: boolean | null,
  status?: string | null,
  failure_reason?: string | null | null,
  verified_at?: string | null | null,
  last_checked_at?: string | null | null,
  dkim_selector?: string | null,
  ownership_token?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="dns_records full type">

```ts
{
  type?: string | null,
  host?: string | null,
  value?: string | null,
  ttl?: number | null,
  purpose?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  is_required?: boolean | null,
  is_inkbox_branded?: boolean | null,
  observed_values?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="apex_mx_warning full type">

```ts
{
  current_mx_targets?: string[] | null,
  acknowledged?: boolean | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="spf_lookup_report full type">

```ts
{
  current?: number | null,
  limit?: number | null,
  mechanisms?: string[] | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### delete

`domains.delete`

Deletes a custom sending domain: sending and receiving stop at once, and it can be restored for 24 hours before deletion is permanent. Fails while mailboxes still use it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.domains.delete({ domain_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `domain_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### forceDelete

`domains.forceDelete`

Deletes a custom sending domain (`domain_id`) by force, bypassing the normal delete flow and its restore window; treat it as permanent.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.domains.forceDelete({ domain_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `domain_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### list

`domains.list`

Lists the organization's custom email sending domains, optionally filtered by lifecycle `status`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.domains.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `not_started \| awaiting_ownership \| pending \| dns_invalid \| verifying \| verified \| failed \| pending_dkim_rotation \| degraded \| pending_deletion \| null` | No | Filter to rows in this lifecycle status. Omit to return every row in the org regardless of state. The mailbox-create dropdown reads from `?status=verified`. |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  organization_id?: string | null,
  domain?: string | null,
  mail_from_subdomain?: string | null,
  is_default?: boolean | null,
  status?: string | null,
  failure_reason?: string | null | null,
  verified_at?: string | null | null,
  last_checked_at?: string | null | null,
  dkim_selector?: string | null,
  ownership_token?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### rotateDkim

`domains.rotateDkim`

Starts a DKIM key rotation for a custom sending domain and returns the new selector and TXT value to publish; the old key stays active until the new one verifies.

| `new_dkim_txt_value` | `string \| null` | No | — |

```ts
{

  is_default?: boolean | null,
  status?: string | null,
  failure_reason?: string | null | null,
  verified_at?: string | null | null,
  last_checked_at?: string | null | null,

} | null
```

### setDefault

`domains.setDefault`

Makes a verified custom domain (`domain_name`) the organization's default for new mailboxes; pass `inkboxmail.com` to revert. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.domains.setDefault({ domain_name: 'mail.example.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `domain_name` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `default_domain` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Bare domain name now serving as the org's default for new mailbox creations. ``None`` means no custom default is set and mailboxes fall back to the platform domain. |

## Hosted Chat

### answerInteraction

`hostedChat.answerInteraction`

Answers a pending tool approval or question in a hosted-agent conversation with `response` (for example approve_once or deny) at `expected_version`; only the first valid answer counts.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedChat.answerInteraction({
	conversation_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	interaction_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	expected_version: 1,
	response: { kind: 'approval', decision: 'approve_once' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `interaction_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expected_version` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `response` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="response full type">

```ts
{
  kind: approval,
  decision: approve_once | deny
} | {
  kind: question,
  answers: Record<string, string | string[]>
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### turns.create

`hostedChat.turns.create`

Sends a message (`content`: text and file parts) to an identity's hosted agent, starting a turn with an optional `model`; wakes a stopped computer, one turn at a time. Admin API key only.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedChat.turns.create({
	conversation_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	content: [{ text: 'Thanks, your order ships tomorrow.' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `model` | `gpt-6-luna \| gpt-6.1-terra \| gpt-6.1-sol \| gpt-6-astra \| claude-haiku-5-5 \| claude-sonnet-5-5 \| claude-opus-5-5 \| claude-fable-5-2 \| deepseek-flash \| deepseek-v4-pro \| null` | No | — |
| `content` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="content full type">

```ts
(
  {
    type?: text,
    text: string
  } | {
    type?: file,
    file_id: string
  }
)[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `terminal` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `blocker` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="blocker full type">

```ts
{
  code?: string | null,
  message?: string | null,
  scope?: string | null,
  retryable?: boolean | null,
  actions?: string[] | null
} | null | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### environment.get

### environment.update

`hostedWorkspace.environment.update`

Sets (`set`) or removes (`remove`) environment variables on the hosted agent's computer at `expected_version`. Changes apply to new processes.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedWorkspace.environment.update({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', expected_version: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expected_version` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `set` | `object` | No | — |
| `remove` | `string[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="set full type">

```ts
Record<string, {
  value: string,
  secret?: boolean
}>
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `applies_to` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### ports.rotateToken

`hostedWorkspace.ports.rotateToken`

Issues a new access token URL for an exposed port on the hosted agent; the old token stops working immediately. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedWorkspace.ports.rotateToken({ port: 8080, agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `port` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | Send back on update to avoid overwriting a concurrent change. |
| `default_port` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `ports` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="ports full type">

```ts
{
  port?: number | null,
  public?: boolean | null,
  url?: string | null | null,
  token_url?: string | null | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### ports.update

`hostedWorkspace.ports.update`

Sets which ports the hosted agent exposes (`ports`, public or token-protected) and the `default_port`, given the current `version`. Removing a port deletes its token.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedWorkspace.ports.update({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', version: 1 });
```

**Input**

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `version` | `number` | Yes | The ``version`` last read; a changed version returns 409 ``stale_version``. |
| `default_port` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `ports` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  public?: boolean
}[] | null
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | Send back on update to avoid overwriting a concurrent change. |
| `default_port` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `ports` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="ports full type">

```ts
{
  port?: number | null,
  public?: boolean | null,
  url?: string | null | null,
  token_url?: string | null | null
}[] | null
```

### resize

`hostedWorkspace.resize`

Changes a stopped hosted computer's CPU and memory (`shape_id`) and optionally `disk_bytes`, given `expected_version`. Runs asynchronously; larger shapes need a paid plan.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedWorkspace.resize({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', shape_id: 'v1-cpu2-ram4gb', expected_version: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `shape_id` | `v1-cpu2-ram4gb \| v1-cpu4-ram8gb` | Yes | v1-cpu4-ram8gb is available on paid plans. |
| `expected_version` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `disk_bytes` | `number \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `operation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `snapshot_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### runAction

`hostedWorkspace.runAction`

Starts, stops, pauses or resumes the hosted agent's computer (`action`) and returns an `operation_id` to poll; stop ends running programs but keeps files. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hostedWorkspace.runAction({ action: 'start', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `action` | `start \| stop \| pause \| resume` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `operation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### schedules.create

`hostedWorkspace.schedules.create`

Creates a scheduled run for the hosted agent (cron, interval or once, in `fields`) with `expected_revision` 0. Needs a running agent; scheduled runs auto-approve tool requests.

**Risk:** `write`

| `state` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Hosting

### callTool

`hosting.callTool`

Sends a JSON-RPC MCP request (`method`, `params`) to the identity's hosted tools endpoint, for example to list or call the tools its hosted agent uses.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hosting.callTool({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', jsonrpc: '2.0', method: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `jsonrpc` | `2.0` | Yes | — |
| `id` | `string \| number \| null` | No | — |
| `method` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `params` | `Record<string, any>` | No | — |



**Output:** `any`


---

### deliveries.list

`hosting.deliveries.list`

Lists up to 100 incoming-event deliveries to an identity's hosted agent, with state, attempts and last error. Page back with `before`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hosting.deliveries.list({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `before` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_before` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  event?: string | null,
  state?: string | null,
  attempts?: number | null,
  received_at?: string | null,
  last_error?: string | null | null,
  retryable?: boolean | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### get

`hosting.get`

Returns an identity's hosted-agent configuration: harness, compute shape, state, default model, instructions, trigger events and current `generation`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hosting.get({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `machine_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `harness` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `tier` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `shape_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Initial shape preference; workspace.computer.shape_id reports the current allocation. |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `generation` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `applied_generation` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `default_model` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `instructions` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `trigger_events` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `default_trigger_events` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `follow_defaults` | `boolean \| null` | No | Derived equality with default_trigger_events; retained for older clients. |
| `credential_binding_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `idle_stop_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `idle_timeout_seconds` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `last_error` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `quota_blocker` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | The hosted agent's address, e.g. https://&#123;handle&#125;.inkbox.sh. |
| `delivery` | `string \| null` | No | How Inkbox events reach the agent: internally, or over the identity's tunnel. |

```ts
{
  resource?: string | null,
  usage?: number | null,
  limit?: number | null
} | null | null
```

</AccordionGroup>

### set

`hosting.set`

Configures an identity's hosted agent (harness, `shape_id`, `default_model`, `instructions`, `trigger_events`) at `expected_generation`; admin key only. `enabled: false` deletes the computer and its files.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.hosting.set({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', expected_generation: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expected_generation` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `harness` | `codex \| hermes \| claude_code \| opencode \| deepseek \| openclaw \| null` | No | — |
| `tier` | `medium \| null` | No | — |
| `shape_id` | `v1-cpu2-ram4gb \| v1-cpu4-ram8gb \| null` | No | Initial compute shape; defaults to v1-cpu2-ram4gb. v1-cpu4-ram8gb is available on paid plans. Use workspace resize to change an existing computer. |
| `default_model` | `gpt-6-luna \| gpt-6.1-terra \| gpt-6.1-sol \| gpt-6-astra \| claude-haiku-5-5 \| claude-sonnet-5-5 \| claude-opus-5-5 \| claude-fable-5-2 \| deepseek-flash \| deepseek-v4-pro \| null` | No | — |
| `instructions` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `trigger_events` | `(message.received \| message.sent \| message.forwarded \| message.delivered \| message.bounced \| message.failed \| text.received \| text.sent \| text.delivered \| text.delivery_failed \| text.delivery_unconfirmed \| call.ended \| imessage.received \| imessage.reaction_received \| imessage.sent \| imessage.delivered \| imessage.delivery_failed \| message.transcription_finished \| whatsapp.reaction_received \| whatsapp.received \| whatsapp.sent \| whatsapp.delivered \| whatsapp.delivery_failed \| whatsapp.delivery_unconfirmed \| a2a.task.created \| a2a.task.message \| a2a.task.canceled \| a2a.sent_task.updated \| slack.dm_received \| slack.group_dm_received \| slack.channel_message_received \| slack.mention_received \| slack.thread_reply_received \| slack.message_updated \| slack.message_deleted \| slack.reaction_added \| slack.reaction_removed \| slack.member_joined \| slack.member_left \| slack.channel_updated \| slack.file_shared \| slack.file_changed \| slack.file_deleted \| slack.pin_added \| slack.pin_removed \| slack.connection_changed \| slack.message_sent \| slack.message_send_failed \| slack.message_send_unknown \| slack.interaction \| slack.session_stopped)[] \| null` | No | — |
| `follow_defaults` | `boolean \| null` | No | Legacy reset shortcut; true selects the current defaults. No automatic following. |
| `credential_binding_id` | `string \| null` | No | Omit to preserve the current key. Null selects included credits; a UUID selects a saved key. |
| `idle_stop_enabled` | `true \| null` | No | Automatic idle shutdown is required; only true is accepted. |
| `reset_instructions` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `idle_timeout_seconds` | `60 \| 300 \| null` | No | Sleep after 1 or 5 minutes of inactivity. New agents default to 60 seconds. |

---

## Identities

### avatar.delete

`identities.avatar.delete`

Removes an identity's avatar so future contact cards go out without a photo. Succeeds even when no avatar is set.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.avatar.delete({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### avatar.get

`identities.avatar.get`

Downloads an identity's avatar as a 600x600 JPEG image.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.avatar.get({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### avatar.upload

`identities.avatar.upload`

Uploads or replaces an identity's avatar image (`file`, multipart), used as the photo on the contact cards it shares.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.avatar.upload({
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	file: { base64: 'iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==', filename: 'avatar.png', contentType: 'image/png' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `file` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | A file: a public URL to it, or &#123;url, filename?, contentType?&#125;, or &#123;base64, filename?, contentType?&#125; for a small one. |

### contactPermissions.update

`identities.contactPermissions.update`

Updates an identity's legacy permissions for one contact: `emails`/`phones` maps set both directions, `inbound_*`/`outbound_*` maps set one. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.contactPermissions.update({ contact_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

| `outbound_emails` | `Record<string, boolean>` | No | — |

| `outbound_phones` | `Record<string, boolean>` | No | — |
| `profile` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `memories` | `boolean` | No | — |

### create

`identities.create`

Creates an agent identity (`agent_handle`) with its mailbox and tunnel, optionally provisioning a phone number, claiming an iMessage line or attaching vault secrets.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.create({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', display_name: 'Support Agent', description: 'Answers customer email for the support team.' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | Agent handle for the identity. A leading '@' is stripped automatically and the handle must be globally unique. |
| `display_name` | `string \| null` | No | Human-readable display name for the identity. Defaults to agent_handle if omitted. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | Free-form description of what the identity is. Visible only to your org. |
| `imessage_enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether this identity can be reached through the shared iMessage service. Defaults to false; set true to opt in. |
| `whatsapp_enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether this identity can be reached through WhatsApp. Defaults to false; set true to opt in. Paid plans include WhatsApp through shared numbers on one agent; other WhatsApp access is invite-only while in beta. |
| `contact_sharing_enabled` | `boolean` | No | Whether an attached dedicated iMessage line automatically shares this identity's name and optional avatar. Defaults to true. |
| `claim_imessage_number` | `true \| null` | No | Claim and attach a dedicated iMessage line during identity creation. Requires imessage_enabled=true. |
| `mailbox` | `object \| null` | No | Optional mailbox-spec; the mailbox is always created. Omit to default to the org's sending domain with a server-chosen local part. |
| `tunnel` | `object \| null` | No | Optional tunnel-spec. Unless `hosted` is true, the tunnel is provisioned with name = agent_handle; this body only opts into PASSTHROUGH or sets a description. |
| `hosted` | `boolean` | No | Create the identity for an Inkbox-hosted agent. Hosted agents receive events without a tunnel, so none is created and `tunnel` must be omitted. |
| `phone_number` | `object \| null` | No | Optional phone-number provisioning payload to create and link a phone number to the identity during identity creation. |
| `vault_secret_ids` | `string \| string[] \| * \| all \| null` | No | Optional vault secret attachment request to scope vault secret access to the newly created agent identity. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="mailbox full type">

```ts
{
  email_local_part?: string | null,
  sending_domain?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="tunnel full type">

```ts
{
  tls_mode?: edge | passthrough
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="phone_number full type">

```ts
{
  type?: local | toll_free,
  state?: string | null,
  incoming_call_action?: auto_accept | auto_reject | webhook | hosted_agent | forward,
  client_websocket_url?: string | null,
  incoming_call_webhook_url?: string | null,
  forwarding_target_type?: phone | sip | null,
  forwarding_phone_number?: string | null,
  forwarding_sip_uri?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>
