### createTunnel

`identities.createTunnel`

Creates the tunnel for an identity that has none (for example one created with `hosted: true`), named after its handle. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.createTunnel({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `tunnel_name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Identifier of the identity associated with this tunnel, when available. |
| `tls_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `cert_pem` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `cert_fingerprint_sha256` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `cert_expires_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `last_connected_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `last_connected_ip_addr` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Caller IP (leftmost X-Forwarded-For hop, falling back to the direct peer) recorded at the most recent /_system/hello. |
| `last_disconnected_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Best-effort timestamp of the most recently observed transition from connected to disconnected. Null means no disconnect has been recorded. Use ``currently_connected`` for current liveness. |
| `currently_connected` | `boolean \| null` | No | Live agent-connection state at read time: true if at least one agent connection is registered in Redis. Always resolved live on every tunnels endpoint — runtime state never appears as a stale placeholder. Identity payloads embed ``TunnelSummaryResponse`` instead, which carries no liveness. |
| `public_host` | `string \| null` | No | Per-tunnel public hostname, e.g. ``my-agent.inkboxwire.com`` in production (non-production environments include the environment label in the tunnel zone, e.g. ``my-agent.beta.inkboxwire.com``). Use this for constructing the public URL the third party hits. |
| `zone` | `string \| null` | No | Tunnel zone host, e.g. ``inkboxwire.com`` in production. Use this as the data-plane h2 endpoint — the agent connects to ``https://{zone}/_system/connect``, not to ``https://{public_host}/_system/connect``. |
| `metadata` | `object \| null` | No | Customer-supplied free-form metadata, set via ``PATCH /tunnels/{id}`` with ``metadata={...}``. Returned as-is on read. Empty dict (``{}``) when unset or cleared. Capped at 4 KB serialized JSON; over-limit writes return 422. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="metadata full type">

```ts
{
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### delete

`identities.delete`

Deletes an identity with its mailbox and tunnel, revokes its scoped API keys and releases its phone number to the carrier. Cannot be undone; the handle stays reserved.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.delete({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### mailContactRules.create

`identities.mailContactRules.create`

Creates an email allow or block rule (`action`) for an identity matching an exact address or domain (`match_type`, `match_target`), optionally per `direction`. Admin API key only.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.mailContactRules.create({
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	action: 'allow',
	match_type: 'exact_email',
	match_target: 'example.com',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both` | No | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block` | Yes | — |
| `match_type` | `exact_email \| domain` | Yes | — |
| `match_target` | `string` | Yes | What the rule matches against. For EXACT_EMAIL: a full email address (stored lowercased). For DOMAIN: a bare domain like 'gmail.com'. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contact` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `match_target` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### mailContactRules.delete

`identities.mailContactRules.delete`

Deletes an email contact rule from an identity. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.mailContactRules.delete({ rule_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `rule_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### phoneContactRules.create

### releasePhoneNumber

`identities.releasePhoneNumber`

Releases an identity's phone number back to the carrier; it is removed from your account and cannot be reassigned. The identity stays active.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.releasePhoneNumber({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### signingKey.createOrRotate

`identities.signingKey.createOrRotate`

Creates the identity's webhook signing key, or replaces the existing one so payloads are signed with the new key. The key is returned only once.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.signingKey.createOrRotate({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `signing_key` | `string \| null` | No | Plaintext signing key (shown once — store it securely) |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | When the key was created (or rotated) |



---

### signingKey.getStatus

`identities.signingKey.getStatus`

Reports whether an identity has a webhook signing key configured and when it was created, without returning the secret.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.signingKey.getStatus({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `configured` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the caller's organization has a signing key configured |
| `created_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | When the key was created (or last rotated), if configured |

### slackContactRules.create

```ts
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	action: 'allow',
	match_type: 'exact_user',
	match_target: 'example.com',
});
```

| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both` | No | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block` | Yes | — |
| `match_type` | `exact_user \| workspace` | Yes | — |

### update

`identities.update`

Updates an identity's display name, description, iMessage and WhatsApp reachability, attached iMessage line, and whitelist/blacklist filter modes per channel.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.identities.update({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `display_name` | `string \| null` | No | New human-readable display name for the identity. Omit to leave unchanged; send null to clear. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | New free-form description of what the identity is. Omit to leave unchanged; send null to clear. |
| `imessage_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this identity can be reached through the shared iMessage service. Omit to leave unchanged. |
| `whatsapp_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this identity can be reached through WhatsApp shared numbers and routers. Omit to leave unchanged. |
| `contact_sharing_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether an attached dedicated iMessage line automatically shares this identity's name and optional avatar. Omit to leave unchanged. |
| `imessage_number_id` | `string \| null` | No | Dedicated iMessage number to attach. Send a number UUID to attach or atomically swap from the current dedicated number, null to move to the shared service, or omit to leave unchanged. Available to admin-scoped API keys and JWT callers. |
| `claim_imessage_number` | `true \| null` | No | Claim and attach a dedicated iMessage line. Omit to avoid provisioning. Requires an Idempotency-Key header when supplied. |
| `imessage_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Alias of phone_filter_mode for the shared SMS, calls, and iMessage list. Manage with admin credentials or through the Console; omit to leave unchanged. |
| `mail_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Whitelist/blacklist mode for this identity's mail contact rules. Manage with admin credentials or through the Console; omit to leave unchanged. |
| `phone_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Whitelist/blacklist mode for this identity's SMS, calls, and iMessage list. Manage with admin credentials or through the Console; omit to leave unchanged. |
| `slack_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Shared Slack whitelist or blacklist mode; resets directional overrides. |
| `slack_inbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Slack inbound whitelist or blacklist mode. |
| `slack_outbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Slack outbound whitelist or blacklist mode. |
| `mail_inbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Email receiving mode. Omit to leave unchanged; null is not accepted. |
| `mail_outbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Email sending mode. Omit to leave unchanged; null is not accepted. |
| `phone_inbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Receiving mode for calls, SMS and iMessage. Omit to leave unchanged; null is not accepted. |
| `phone_outbound_filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Sending mode for calls, SMS and iMessage. Omit to leave unchanged; null is not accepted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `notices` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `display_name` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `whatsapp_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `whatsapp_configured` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `whatsapp_number` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `imessage_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `contact_sharing_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `imessage_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `mail_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phone_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `slack_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `slack_inbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `slack_outbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `mail_inbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `mail_outbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Imessage

### assignments.list

`imessage.assignments.list`

Lists active iMessage connections (one per recipient connected to an agent through the router or connect call), newest first, optionally for one `agent_identity_id`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.assignments.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | Narrow to one agent identity. Ignored for agent-scoped keys (always their own identity). |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `offset` | `number` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  remote_number?: string | null,
  agent_identity_id?: string | null,
  organization_id?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  released_at?: string | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### assignments.release

`imessage.assignments.release`

Disconnects a recipient from an agent (`assignment_id`): their messages stop reaching the agent and sends into that conversation fail. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.assignments.release({ assignment_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `assignment_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### checkCapability

`imessage.checkCapability`

Checks which messaging service (`iMessage`, `SMS` or `RCS`) is currently available for an E.164 phone `number`. Read-only; sends nothing.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.checkCapability({ number: '+14155550123' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `number` | `string` | Yes | Recipient phone number in E.164 format. |



**Output:** `string`


---

### connect

`imessage.connect`

Connects a recipient (`recipient_number`) to an agent identity over a shared iMessage line, and texts them `first_message` if given. Phone contact rules must allow them.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.connect({ agent_identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', recipient_number: '+14155550123' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `recipient_number` | `string` | Yes | Phone number in E.164 format (e.g. '+15551234567'). |
| `first_message` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `assignment` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `assigned_number` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Assigned E.164 number for an organization-owned shared pool or personal dedicated line; otherwise null. |
| `message` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `already_connected` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="assignment full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  remote_number?: string | null,
  agent_identity_id?: string | null,
  organization_id?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  released_at?: string | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
} | null
```

### contactRules.create

`imessage.contactRules.create`

Creates an allow or block rule for an E.164 number (`match_target`) on an identity's shared phone list (iMessage, SMS, calls, WhatsApp), optionally per `direction`. Admin API key only.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.contactRules.create({
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	action: 'allow',
	match_type: 'exact_number',
	match_target: '+14155550123',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both` | No | — |
| `action` | `allow \| block` | Yes | — |
| `match_type` | `exact_number` | No | — |
| `match_target` | `string` | Yes | Phone number to match, in E.164 format. |

| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### disconnect

`imessage.disconnect`

Disconnects the active iMessage connection between an agent identity (`agent_identity_id`) and a recipient (`recipient_number`).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.disconnect({ agent_identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', recipient_number: '+14155550123' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `recipient_number` | `string` | Yes | Phone number in E.164 format (e.g. '+15551234567'). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `assignment_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `disconnected` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

### getMediaPlayback

`imessage.getMediaPlayback`

Returns an iMessage audio attachment (`media_id`) converted to WAV for browser playback.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.getMediaPlayback({ media_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `media_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### messages.send

`imessage.messages.send`

Sends an iMessage from an identity to `to` (1 to 8 E.164 numbers; 2 or more is a group) or into `conversation_id`, with `text`, media, optional reply target, send style or voice note.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.messages.send({ agent_identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', to: '+14155550123', text: 'Your table for two is booked for 7pm.' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | Agent identity to send as. Required for org-wide callers when sending by `to`; ignored for agent-scoped keys. |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `prefer` | `string \| null` | No | Use idempotency-replay with Idempotency-Key to replay the original send result. |
| `to` | `string \| string[] \| null` | No | One recipient or a list of 1–8 recipients. Lists with two or more members start or select a best-known exact-membership group. Mutually exclusive with conversation_id. |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | Existing conversation to reply into. Mutually exclusive with to. |
| `reply_to_message_id` | `string \| null` | No | Message to reply to within conversation_id. Omit for an ordinary message. |
| `plain_reply_fallback` | `boolean` | No | Allow an ordinary message in the same conversation when, before sending, the reply target is known to use SMS, RCS, or downgraded transport, or is a text-only inbound reaction notice that cannot anchor a native reply. Set false to require a native reply. A native reply the provider definitely rejects is resent once as an ordinary message; an uncertain one is not. Ignored without reply_to_message_id. |
| `text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `media_urls` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `staged_media` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `send_style` | `celebration \| shooting_star \| fireworks \| lasers \| love \| confetti \| balloons \| spotlight \| echo \| invisible \| gentle \| loud \| slam \| null` | No | — |
| `send_as_voice_note` | `boolean` | No | Speak text in the agent's hosted voice and send it as a native iMessage voice note instead of text. Text is required, is limited to 600 characters, and cannot be combined with media. |
| `fallback_to_text` | `boolean` | No | When a voice note can't be produced before the message is accepted, for example because the text is longer than 600 characters, send the text as an ordinary message instead. Set false to fail the request. Ignored unless send_as_voice_note is true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="staged_media full type">

```ts
{
  handle: string,
  content_hash: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

```ts
  status?: string | null
}
```

### numbers.claim

`imessage.numbers.claim`

Claims one unattached dedicated iMessage line for the organization. Requires an admin API key; the plugin sends a fresh `idempotencyKey` unless you pass one.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.numbers.claim({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `line_role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inbound_only` | `boolean \| null` | No | Deprecated compatibility field; dedicated lines can initiate. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### reactions.remove

`imessage.reactions.remove`

Removes a tapback the agent sent (`reaction_id`) from the person's message.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.reactions.remove({ reaction_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `reaction_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |



---

### reactions.send

`imessage.reactions.send`

Sends a tapback (`reaction`) on a message a person sent the agent (`message_id`). The agent cannot react to its own messages.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.reactions.send({ message_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', reaction: 'love' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `message_id` | `string` | Yes | Received message ID. A media-only attachment resolves to its original multipart message and part; the response identifies that canonical target. For text with attachments, use the original message ID and an explicit part_index. |
| `reaction` | `love \| like \| dislike \| laugh \| emphasize \| question \| eyes` | Yes | — |
| `part_index` | `number` | No | Part of the original multipart message. Use 0 for an individual attachment message ID; its part is resolved automatically. |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  provider_message_handle?: string | null | null,
  id?: string | null,
  conversation_id?: string | null,
  assignment_id?: string | null | null,
  target_message_id?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  reaction?: string | null,
  custom_emoji?: string | null | null,
  remote_number?: string | null,
  part_index?: number | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
} | {
  reaction_id?: string | null,
  message_id?: string | null,
  reaction?: string | null,
  target_message_id?: string | null | null,
  part_index?: number | null | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### router.getAvatar

`imessage.router.getAvatar`

Downloads the avatar image of the organization's iMessage router.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.router.getAvatar({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.imessage.uploadMedia({
	file: { base64: 'iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==', filename: 'avatar.png', contentType: 'image/png' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `file` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | A file: a public URL to it, or &#123;url, filename?, contentType?&#125;, or &#123;base64, filename?, contentType?&#125; for a small one. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="file full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `media_url` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `content_type` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `size` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |



---

## Inbound

### checkSignature

`inbound.checkSignature`

Tests whether a sample request (`headers`, `body`) passes signature settings (`signature`) or a saved source's settings (`source_id`). Stores nothing.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.inbound.checkSignature({
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	headers: {},
	body: 'Customer prefers email over phone calls.',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `signature` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `source_id` | `string \| null` | No | Check against this source's saved settings instead. |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string>` | Yes | — |
| `body` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `body_encoding` | `utf8 \| base64` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="signature full type">

```ts
{
  secret_encoding?: text | base64,
  header: string,
  format?: whole | prefix | list,
  format_value?: string | null,
  algorithm?: sha256 | sha1 | sha512,
  encoding?: hex | base64,
  signed_content?: string,
  timestamp_from?: none | header | list,
  timestamp_value?: string | null,
  max_age_seconds?: number | null,
  secret: string
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `verified` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `reason` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Why verification failed; null when it passed. |



---


**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `source_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `event_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `received_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `attempts` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `last_error` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `signature_verified` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `content_type` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `size_bytes` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `request` | `object \| null` | No | — |

```ts
  headers?: Record<string, string> | null,

  body?: string | null,
  body_encoding?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### events.reportResult

`inbound.events.reportResult`

Reports that the agent could not handle an inbound event delivery (`status` failed, `error`, `delivery_id`) so it can be found and replayed. Needs the identity's own key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.inbound.events.reportResult({
	source_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	event_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	agent_handle: 'support-agent',
	status: 'failed',
	error: 'example',
	delivery_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `source_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `event_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `status` | `failed` | Yes | — |
| `error` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `delivery_id` | `string` | Yes | The ``delivery_id`` of the delivery being reported. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### sources.create

`inbound.sources.create`

Creates an inbound webhook source (`name`) for a hosted agent and returns the URL third parties post events to, with optional `signature` checking and `handler` (agent or script).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.inbound.sources.create({ agent_handle: 'support-agent', name: 'Support inbox' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `signature` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `handler` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="signature full type">

```ts
{
  secret_encoding?: text | base64,
  header: string,
  format?: whole | prefix | list,
  format_value?: string | null,
  algorithm?: sha256 | sha1 | sha512,
  encoding?: hex | base64,
  signed_content?: string,
  timestamp_from?: none | header | list,
  timestamp_value?: string | null,
  max_age_seconds?: number | null,
  secret: string
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="handler full type">

```ts
  type?: agent,
  instructions?: string | null
} | {
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

### drafts.create

`mail.drafts.create`

Creates an email draft in a mailbox (`email_address`) with optional `recipients`, subject, text/HTML body, attachments, or reply/forward fields. Does not send.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.drafts.create({
	email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com',
	recipients: {
		to: ['ada@example.com'],
	},
	subject: 'Quote for 20 seats',
	body_text: 'Hi Ada, here is the quote you asked for.',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `recipients` | `object` | No | — |
| `subject` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body_text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body_html` | `string \| null` | No | — |

| `forward_message_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `forward_mode` | `inline \| wrapped` | No | — |
| `include_original_attachments` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `forward_note_text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `forward_note_html` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### drafts.delete

`mail.drafts.delete`

Deletes a draft at the given `generation` permanently and frees its storage. A draft that is being sent cannot be deleted.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.drafts.delete({ draft_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com', generation: 1 });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `generation` | `number` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### drafts.getAttachment

`mail.drafts.getAttachment`

Downloads one attachment of a draft by `part_index` at a given `generation`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.drafts.getAttachment({
	draft_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	part_index: 1,
	email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com',
	generation: 1,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `draft_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `part_index` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `generation` | `number` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### drafts.send

`mail.drafts.send`

Sends a draft revision (`generation`) as an email to its recipients, applying the mailbox signature at send time. Delivers to outside recipients and cannot be undone.

### listOrgContactRules

`mail.listOrgContactRules`

Lists email contact rules across every identity in the organization, optionally for one `agent_identity_id`, filterable by `action`, `match_type` and `direction`. Admin API key only.


| `match_type` | `exact_email \| domain \| null` | No | Filter by exact_email or domain. |
| `direction` | `inbound \| outbound \| both \| null` | No | Filter by applicable direction; both selects only bidirectional rules. |

| `offset` | `number` | No | Number of rules to skip. |

### mailboxes.update

`mail.mailboxes.update`

Updates a mailbox's contact-rule `filter_mode` and email signature (`signature_html`, `signature_text`, `signature_enabled`). The address cannot change.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.mailboxes.update({ email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Contact-rule filter mode. 'whitelist' = only counterparties matching an active rule are allowed; 'blacklist' = all counterparties are allowed except those matching an active rule. Applies symmetrically to inbound receipt and outbound send. Modifying this field requires an admin-scoped API key. Omit to leave unchanged; explicit null is rejected. |
| `signature_html` | `string \| null` | No | HTML signature fragment. Sanitized before saving. Null clears it; omitted fields are unchanged. When supplied without signature_text, a plain-text fallback is generated. Images require absolute HTTPS URLs. |
| `signature_text` | `string \| null` | No | Plain-text signature. Null clears it; omitted fields are unchanged unless HTML is updated. When null, sending derives text from signature_html if present. |
| `signature_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Automatically insert the saved signature when the organization has custom signatures enabled. Defaults to false. Omit to leave unchanged; null is rejected. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `notices` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `email_address` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sending_domain` | `string \| null` | No | Bare domain the mailbox sends from, derived from email_address. Matches the platform default (inkboxmail.com) or the customer's verified custom domain. |
| `filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `outbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the agent identity that owns this mailbox, or null on tombstones / system mailboxes. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `storage_used_bytes` | `number \| null` | No | Live storage used by this inbox, in bytes. |
| `storage_limit_bytes` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Resolved plan-tiered storage cap in bytes; null when the route did not resolve it (the ORM does not carry the cap). |
| `signature_html` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `signature_text` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `signature_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `filter_mode_change_notice` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `unread_count` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |

### messages.forward

`mail.messages.forward`

Forwards a stored message from a mailbox to `recipients` as a new thread, with optional note, extra attachments and original attachments. Sends real email.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.messages.forward({
	message_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com',
	recipients: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `prefer` | `string \| null` | No | Use idempotency-replay with Idempotency-Key to replay the original send result. |
| `recipients` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `mode` | `inline \| wrapped` | No | — |
| `subject` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body_text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body_html` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_attachments` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_staged_attachments` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `include_original_attachments` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `reply_to` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `track_opens` | `boolean` | No | Embed an open-tracking pixel in the forward's HTML body. The outgoing forward must have an HTML part (the caller note in wrapped mode; caller note or original HTML in inline mode); rejected with 422 otherwise. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="recipients full type">

```ts
{
  to?: string[] | null,
  cc?: string[] | null,
  bcc?: string[] | null
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="additional_attachments full type">

```ts
{
  filename: string,
  content_type: string,
  content_base64: string,
  content_id?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="additional_staged_attachments full type">

```ts
{
  media_handle: string,
  filename: string,
  content_hash: string,
  content_type: string,
  content_id?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `mailbox_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `message_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `from_address` | `string \| null` | No | — |

| `to_addresses` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |

| `subject` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `snippet` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### messages.send

`mail.messages.send`

Sends an email from a mailbox (`email_address`) to `recipients` (to/cc/bcc) with `subject`, text or HTML body, optional attachments, `reply_to` and threading. Delivers real mail.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.messages.send({
	email_address: 'support-agent@inkboxmail.com',
	recipients: {
		to: ['ada@example.com'],
	},
	subject: 'Your order has shipped',
	body_text: 'Hi Ada, your order shipped today. Tracking: 1Z999AA10123456784.',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `email_address` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `prefer` | `string \| null` | No | Use idempotency-replay with Idempotency-Key to replay the original send result. |
| `recipients` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `subject` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `body_text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body_html` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_id` | `string \| null` | No | Attach this message to an existing conversation. Preferred over in_reply_to_message_id when replying; pass the thread_id returned by the list/get endpoints. |
| `in_reply_to_message_id` | `string \| null` | No | Message-ID of the email being replied to |
| `attachments` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `staged_attachments` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `reply_to` | `string \| null` | No | Optional Reply-To address. If set, recipients' mail clients route replies here instead of the From mailbox. |
| `track_opens` | `boolean` | No | Embed an open-tracking pixel in the HTML body. Requires body_html; a send with track_opens and no HTML body is rejected with 422. Opens surface as first_opened_at/open_count on the message. Note: tracking pixels can raise spam scores. |

```ts
}
```

```ts
  content_type: string,
  content_base64: string,
  content_id?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="staged_attachments full type">

```ts
{
  media_handle: string,

  content_type: string,

}[] | null
```

### visibleMessages.delete

`mail.visibleMessages.delete`

Deletes a message by UUID from whichever mailbox visible to the API key holds it.

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.mail.visibleMessages.delete({ message_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

## Message Sends

### lookup

`messageSends.lookup`

Recovers the message ID of an earlier send (email, SMS, iMessage or WhatsApp) from its idempotency key, `sender_kind`, `sender_id` and `operation`. Read-only; sends nothing.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.messageSends.lookup({
	sender_kind: 'mailbox',
	sender_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	operation: 'mail.send',
	idempotencyKey: 'send-2026-11-23-001',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sender_kind` | `mailbox \| phone_number \| imessage_identity \| whatsapp_identity` | Yes | — |
| `sender_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `operation` | `mail.send \| mail.reply \| mail.reply_all \| mail.forward \| text.send \| imessage.send \| whatsapp.send` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | Yes | The Idempotency-Key the earlier request was sent with. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `message_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### create

`notes.create`

Creates a note with a `body` and optional `title`; the creator is recorded from the API key.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts

```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `title` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `body` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_by` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `title` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `body` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `access` | `object[] \| null` | No | Raw ``note_access`` rows for this note, inlined so the console can skip a follow-up ``GET /notes/{id}/access`` per row. Empty when the note has zero grants (typical for human-authored notes); not a 'not loaded' sentinel — every response path populates this field. |

```ts
  id?: string | null,

  created_at?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### calls.place

`phone.calls.place`

Places an outbound phone call to `to_number`, bridged to your `client_websocket_url` or run by Inkbox Voice AI (`mode`). Rings a real phone and uses call minutes.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.calls.place({ from_number: '+14155550100', to_number: '+14155550123' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `async_command` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `origination` | `dedicated_number \| shared_imessage_number \| dedicated_imessage_number` | No | — |
| `from_number` | `string \| null` | No | Source phone number, in E.164 format. Required for dedicated_number origination. |
| `to_number` | `string` | Yes | Destination phone number to dial, in E.164 format. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | Agent identity placing the call (required for shared_imessage_number under admin/JWT). |
| `client_websocket_url` | `string \| null` | No | WebSocket URL (wss://) for audio bridging. Falls back to phone number's client_websocket_url. |
| `mode` | `client_websocket \| hosted_agent` | No | — |
| `hosted_agent_authority_mode` | `contact_scoped \| yolo \| null` | No | Optional hosted-agent data-scope override: contact_scoped or yolo. Omit to inherit the identity's saved default. |
| `outbound_greeting_wait_seconds` | `number \| null` | No | Seconds to wait for recipient speech before a proactive hosted outbound greeting. Omit or send null for 1.5 seconds; 0 opens immediately. Only valid with mode=hosted_agent. Recipient speech cancels the proactive greeting; this is not a delay before every reply. |
| `on_voicemail` | `hang_up \| leave_message \| ignore \| null` | No | What to do when the call reaches voicemail. leave_message (default with mode=hosted_agent) has the hosted agent leave a message after the beep, then end the call; hang_up (default otherwise) ends the call once a voicemail beep is detected; ignore turns detection off and treats whatever answers as a person. |
| `voicemail_message` | `string \| null` | No | What the hosted agent should say when leaving a voicemail. Only valid with on_voicemail=leave_message; when omitted, the agent composes a short message from reason. |
| `voicemail_detection` | `enabled \| disabled` | No | — |
| `reason` | `string \| null` | No | Task brief for the hosted agent, e.g. what to accomplish on the call. Required with mode=hosted_agent; invalid otherwise. |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  origin?: string | null,
  local_phone_number?: string | null | null,
  remote_phone_number?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  client_websocket_url?: string | null | null,
  mode?: string | null,
  hosted_agent_authority_mode?: string | null,
  voicemail_detection?: string | null,
  on_voicemail?: string | null | null,
  reason?: string | null | null,
  post_call_action_items?: {
    id?: string | null,
    seq?: number | null,
    action?: string | null,
    details?: string | null | null,
    status?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  connections?: {
    id?: string | null,
    kind?: string | null,
    trigger?: string | null,
    status?: string | null,
    target_type?: string | null,
    target?: string | null,
    requested_at?: string | null,
    dialing_at?: string | null | null,
    connected_at?: string | null | null,
    ended_at?: string | null | null,
    failure_code?: string | null | null
  }[] | null,
  forwardings?: {
    id?: string | null,
    trigger?: string | null,
    status?: string | null,
    target_type?: string | null,
    target?: string | null,
    requested_at?: string | null,
    dialing_at?: string | null | null,
    forwarded_at?: string | null | null,
    ended_at?: string | null | null,
    failure_code?: string | null | null
  }[] | null,
  use_inkbox_tts?: boolean | null | null,
  use_inkbox_stt?: boolean | null | null,
  hangup_reason?: string | null | null,
  ended_by?: string | null | null,
  provider_hangup_cause?: string | null | null,
  started_at?: string | null | null,
  ended_at?: string | null | null,
  is_blocked?: boolean | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null,
  rate_limit?: {
    calls_used?: number | null,
    calls_remaining?: number | null,
    calls_limit?: number | null,
    minutes_used?: number | null,
    minutes_remaining?: number | null,
    minutes_limit?: number | null
  } | null
} | {
  call_id?: string | null,
  state?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### media.getPlayback

`phone.media.getPlayback`

Returns an audio attachment from a text message (`media_id`) converted for browser playback.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.media.getPlayback({ media_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `media_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### numbers.provision

`phone.numbers.provision`

Provisions a new local phone number from carrier inventory (optional US `state`) and assigns it to an identity (`agent_handle`). Returns 429 once the organization reaches its active-number cap.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.numbers.provision({ agent_handle: 'support-agent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_handle` | `string` | Yes | Agent identity handle to assign this number to (e.g. 'sales-agent' or '@sales-agent'). |
| `type` | `local \| toll_free` | No | — |
| `state` | `string \| null` | No | US state abbreviation (e.g. 'NY', 'CA'). Only used for local numbers. |
| `incoming_call_action` | `auto_accept \| auto_reject \| webhook \| hosted_agent \| forward` | No | — |
| `client_websocket_url` | `string \| null` | No | WebSocket URL (wss://) for audio bridging. Required when incoming_call_action is 'auto_accept'. |
| `incoming_call_webhook_url` | `string \| null` | No | Webhook URL for incoming call notifications. Required when incoming_call_action is 'webhook'. |
| `forwarding_target_type` | `phone \| sip \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_phone_number` | `string \| null` | No | Phone number in E.164 format (e.g. '+15551234567'). |
| `forwarding_sip_uri` | `string \| null` | No | Complete SIP destination in sip:user@host format. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `notices` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_error_detail` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_ready_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `incoming_call_action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `client_websocket_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `incoming_call_webhook_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_target_type` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_phone_number` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_sip_uri` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `outbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `country` | `string \| null` | No | ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code (e.g. 'US' or 'GB'). |
| `state` | `string \| null \| null` | No | 2-letter US state abbreviation for LOCAL numbers (e.g. 'NY'). Null when unknown or not applicable, including numbers without a US state. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the agent identity that owns this phone number, or null if the number is standalone (not tied to any agent). |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `filter_mode_change_notice` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### numbers.release

`phone.numbers.release`

Releases a phone number back to the carrier and removes it from your organization. It cannot be recovered.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.numbers.release({ phone_number_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `phone_number_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | Inkbox accepted the call; it answers with no body. |

### numbers.update

`phone.numbers.update`

Updates a phone number's incoming-call handling (`incoming_call_action`, WebSocket, webhook or forwarding target) and contact-rule `filter_mode`.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.numbers.update({ phone_number_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `phone_number_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `incoming_call_action` | `auto_accept \| auto_reject \| webhook \| hosted_agent \| forward \| null` | No | How to handle inbound calls: auto_accept, auto_reject, webhook, hosted_agent, or forward with one phone or SIP destination. |
| `client_websocket_url` | `string \| null` | No | WebSocket URL (wss://) for audio bridging on auto_accept. |
| `incoming_call_webhook_url` | `string \| null` | No | Webhook URL for incoming call notifications. Set to null to unsubscribe. |
| `forwarding_target_type` | `phone \| sip \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_phone_number` | `string \| null` | No | Phone number in E.164 format (e.g. '+15551234567'). |
| `forwarding_sip_uri` | `string \| null` | No | Complete SIP destination in sip:user@host format. |
| `filter_mode` | `whitelist \| blacklist \| null` | No | Contact-rule filter mode (single field; covers both voice calls and SMS). 'whitelist' = only counterparties matching an active rule are allowed; 'blacklist' = all counterparties are allowed except those matching an active rule. Applies symmetrically to inbound receipt and outbound dial/send. Modifying this field requires an admin-scoped API key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `notices` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `number` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_error_detail` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `sms_ready_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `incoming_call_action` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `client_websocket_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `incoming_call_webhook_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_target_type` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_phone_number` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `forwarding_sip_uri` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `inbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `outbound_filter_mode` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `country` | `string \| null` | No | ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code (e.g. 'US' or 'GB'). |
| `state` | `string \| null \| null` | No | 2-letter US state abbreviation for LOCAL numbers (e.g. 'NY'). Null when unknown or not applicable, including numbers without a US state. |
| `agent_identity_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | ID of the agent identity that owns this phone number, or null if the number is standalone (not tied to any agent). |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `filter_mode_change_notice` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### textConversations.list

`phone.textConversations.list`

Lists SMS/MMS conversations on a phone number by most recent message, with previews and unread counts; groups only with `include_groups`.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.textConversations.list({ phone_number_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `phone_number_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `offset` | `number` | No | — |
| `include_groups` | `boolean` | No | Default ``false`` hides group conversations from the listing — old clients see only 1:1 threads, byte-for-byte the same as before. Pass ``true`` to also include group threads. |
| `is_blocked` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `participants` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `contact_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `start_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | Filter created_at &gt;= this date/datetime. Bare date =&gt; whole day in tz. |
| `end_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | Filter created_at &lt;= this date/datetime. Bare date =&gt; whole day inclusive. |
| `tz` | `string \| null` | No | IANA zone (e.g. America/New_York) for zone-less values; default UTC. |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  remote_phone_number?: string | null | null,
  id?: string | null | null,
  participants?: string[] | null | null,
  is_group?: boolean | null | null,
  latest_text?: string | null | null,
  latest_direction?: string | null,
  latest_type?: string | null,
  latest_has_media?: boolean | null,
  latest_message_at?: string | null,
  unread_count?: number | null,
  total_count?: number | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### texts.list

`phone.texts.list`

Lists SMS/MMS messages on a phone number, newest first, filterable by read and blocked state and date range.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.texts.list({ phone_number_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `phone_number_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `offset` | `number` | No | — |
| `is_read` | `boolean \| null` | No | Filter by read state: true for read, false for unread, omit for all. |
| `is_blocked` | `boolean \| null` | No | Filter by blocked state: true for only blocked, false for only non-blocked, omit for all. Agent-scoped callers never see blocked rows regardless of this filter. |
| `start_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | Filter created_at &gt;= this date/datetime. Bare date =&gt; whole day in tz. |
| `end_datetime` | `string \| null` | No | Filter created_at &lt;= this date/datetime. Bare date =&gt; whole day inclusive. |
| `tz` | `string \| null` | No | IANA zone (e.g. America/New_York) for zone-less values; default UTC. |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  local_phone_number?: string | null,
  remote_phone_number?: string | null | null,
  text?: string | null | null,

  media?: {
    transcription?: {
      status?: string | null,

      error_code?: string | null | null
    } | null | null,

  origin?: string | null,
```

### texts.send

`phone.texts.send`

Sends an SMS, or MMS with `media_urls`, from a phone number to `to` (1 to 8 numbers; 2 or more is a group) or into `conversation_id`. Texts real phones and uses SMS allowance.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.phone.texts.send({
	phone_number_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7',
	to: ['+14155550123'],
	text: 'Your appointment is confirmed for 3pm tomorrow.',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `phone_number_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string \| null` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |
| `prefer` | `string \| null` | No | Use idempotency-replay with Idempotency-Key to replay the original send result. |
| `to` | `string \| string[] \| null` | No | One recipient (1:1) or a list of 1–8 recipients (1:1 with N=1, or group MMS when N≥2). Each recipient is an E.164 number. Mutually exclusive with ``conversation_id``. |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | Existing conversation to reply into. The server resolves this to the conversation's participants list and sends to all of them. Mutually exclusive with ``to``. |
| `text` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `media_urls` | `string[] \| null` | No | Publicly-fetchable media URLs to attach |
| `staged_media` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `send_as_voice_note` | `boolean` | No | Speak text in the agent's hosted voice and send it as an MP3 audio file in an MMS instead of text. Text is required, is limited to 600 characters, and cannot be combined with media. |
| `fallback_to_text` | `boolean` | No | When a voice note can't be produced before the message is accepted, for example because the text is longer than 600 characters, send the text as an ordinary message instead. Set false to fail the request. Ignored unless send_as_voice_note is true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="staged_media full type">

```ts
{
  handle: string,
  content_hash: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  direction?: string | null,
  local_phone_number?: string | null,
  remote_phone_number?: string | null | null,
  text?: string | null | null,
  type?: string | null,
  media?: {
    transcription?: {
      status?: string | null,
      text?: string | null | null,
      error_code?: string | null | null
    } | null | null,
    id?: string | null | null,
    content_type?: string | null,
    size?: number | null,
    url?: string | null
  }[] | null | null,
  is_read?: boolean | null,
  delivery_status?: string | null | null,
  origin?: string | null,
  error_code?: string | null | null,
  error_detail?: string | null | null,
  sent_at?: string | null | null,
  delivered_at?: string | null | null,
  failed_at?: string | null | null,
  conversation_id?: string | null | null,
  sender_phone_number?: string | null | null,
  recipients?: {
    recipient_phone_number?: string | null,
    delivery_status?: string | null | null,
    carrier?: string | null | null,
    line_type?: string | null | null,
    error_code?: string | null | null,
    error_detail?: string | null | null,
    sent_at?: string | null | null,
    delivered_at?: string | null | null,
    failed_at?: string | null | null
  }[] | null | null,
  is_blocked?: boolean | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
} | {

}
```

## Slack

### actions.get

`slack.actions.get`

Returns the recorded result of a Slack message send (`action_id`): status, conversation, message timestamp or error. Does not contact Slack.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.actions.get({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', action_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `action_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `connection_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `message_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `retry_after` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Immediate retry delay in seconds; null on stored outcomes. |



---

### actions.getByKey

`slack.actions.getByKey`

Looks up a Slack message send by the idempotency key it was sent with, to check whether a timed-out send went through without sending again.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.actions.getByKey({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', idempotencyKey: 'send-2026-11-23-001' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | Yes | The Idempotency-Key the earlier request was sent with. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `connection_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `message_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `retry_after` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Immediate retry delay in seconds; null on stored outcomes. |

### applications.prepare

`slack.applications.prepare`

Starts creating the identity's dedicated Slack app in a saved provisioning workspace (`identity_id`, `provisioning_workspace_id`). Runs in the background; repeat calls reuse it.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.applications.prepare({ identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', provisioning_workspace_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `provisioning_workspace_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `provisioning_workspace_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `retry_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### applications.remove

`slack.applications.remove`

Removes an identity's Slack app from Inkbox and tries to delete it from Slack (`expected_application_generation`, `request_id`); its connection and history become inaccessible. Admin key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.applications.remove({ identity_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', expected_application_generation: 1, request_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `identity_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `expected_application_generation` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `request_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `operation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `deletion_status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `deletion_error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

### archive.backfill

`slack.archive.backfill`

Imports accessible past Slack history for one conversation (`conversation_id`) or thread (`thread_ts`) into a connection's retained archive.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.archive.backfill({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', conversation_id: 'C0123456789' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `restart` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `thread_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `imported_count` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `oldest_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `newest_ts` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `includes_all_threads` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `access_revoked` | `boolean \| null` | No | Last observed membership callback, not current access authority. |

### archive.listMessages

`slack.archive.listMessages`

Lists retained Slack messages from a connection's archive, newest first, by conversation, thread or timestamp range, with optional cached sender, reaction and file details.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.archive.listMessages({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `thread_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `before_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `after_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `latest_per_conversation` | `boolean` | No | Return only the latest matching message per conversation; limit counts conversations. |
| `roots_only` | `boolean` | No | Page roots, broadcasts, and replies with no retained parent. |
| `include` | `string \| null` | No | Optional cached expansions: conversation,sender,reactions,files. |
| `message_id` | `string \| null` | No | Return only this retained message, such as a received event's data.message_id. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `messages` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `page_boundary` | `object \| null` | No | Oldest scanned position for a nonterminal chronological page, before access filtering. Not necessarily a visible message; null for search and exhausted pages. |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `included` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### archive.purge

`slack.archive.purge`

Deletes all retained Slack messages in a connection's archive and cannot be undone; new messages keep being captured. Requires an admin API key.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.archive.purge({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `Record<string, string>`


---

### archive.search

`slack.archive.search`

Searches retained Slack messages in one connection's archive by keyword (`q`), optionally by conversation, user or time range. Not semantic search.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.archive.search({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', q: 'invoice' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `q` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `user_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `after_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `before_ts` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `messages` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `page_boundary` | `object \| null` | No | Oldest scanned position for a nonterminal chronological page, before access filtering. Not necessarily a visible message; null for search and exhausted pages. |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `included` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### conversations.join

`slack.conversations.join`

Makes the identity's Slack bot join a public channel (`conversation_id`).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.inkbox.api.slack.conversations.join({ connection_id: '7c9e6679-7425-40de-944b-e07fc1f90ae7', conversation_id: 'C0123456789' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `idempotencyKey` | `string` | No | Idempotency-Key header: a retry with the same key and body returns what this key already did instead of doing it again. Leave it out and the plugin sends a new random key. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `connection_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `operation` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `conversation_id` | `string \| null` | No | — |

| `file_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `error_code` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `retry_after` | `number \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `processing_status` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |
| `agent_status` | `string \| null \| null` | No | — |

| `status` | `active \| processing \| suspended \| closed` | Yes | — |
